services:
  # 1. Frontend (Web)
  web:
    image: ${DOCKER_REGISTRY:-}archspec-web:${TAG:-latest}
    container_name: archspec-web
    ports:
      - "${WEB_PORT}:80"
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      VITE_CLAWITH_BASE_URL: ${VITE_CLAWITH_BASE_URL}
      VITE_CLAWITH_AGENT_ID: ${VITE_CLAWITH_AGENT_ID}
      VITE_CLAWITH_EMAIL: ${VITE_CLAWITH_EMAIL}
      VITE_CLAWITH_PASSWORD: ${VITE_CLAWITH_PASSWORD}
    networks:
      - archspec-net

  # 2. Backend (Java)
  backend:
    image: ${DOCKER_REGISTRY:-}archspec-backend:${TAG:-latest}
    container_name: archspec-backend
    ports:
      - "${BACKEND_PORT}:8080"
    restart: unless-stopped
    depends_on:
      postgres:
        condition: service_healthy
    volumes:
      - ${LICENSE_FILE:-./license.lic}:/app/license.lic:ro
    environment:
      SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE: ${SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE:-prod}
      SPRING_DATASOURCE_URL: jdbc:postgresql://${POSTGRES_HOST}:5432/${POSTGRES_DB}?currentSchema=public
      SPRING_DATASOURCE_USERNAME: ${POSTGRES_USER}
      SPRING_DATASOURCE_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD}
      APP_DATABASE_INIT_ENABLED: false  # 生产环境禁用自动初始化，手动执行
      APP_BASE_URL: ${APP_BASE_URL:-http://java:8080}
      LICENSE_PATH: /app/license.lic
      JWT_SECRET: ${JWT_SECRET:-[REDACTED_DEFAULT]}
      OSS_ACCESS_KEY_ID: ${OSS_ACCESS_KEY_ID}
      OSS_ACCESS_KEY_SECRET: ${OSS_ACCESS_KEY_SECRET}
      OSS_BUCKET: ${OSS_BUCKET}
      OSS_REGION: ${OSS_REGION:-cn-guangzhou}
      OSS_ENDPOINT: ${OSS_ENDPOINT:-sts.cn-guangzhou.aliyuncs.com}
      OSS_HOST: ${OSS_HOST}
      OSS_UPLOAD_DIR: ${OSS_UPLOAD_DIR:-reviews/}
      OSS_EXPIRE_SECONDS: ${OSS_EXPIRE_SECONDS:-3600}
      OSS_STS_ROLE_ARN: ${OSS_STS_ROLE_ARN:-}
    networks:
      - archspec-net

  # 3. AI Agent (Python)
  agent:
    image: ${DOCKER_REGISTRY:-}archspec-agent:${TAG:-latest}
    container_name: archspec-agent
    ports:
      - "${AGENT_PORT}:5000"
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      - POSTGRES_URL=postgresql://${POSTGRES_USER}:${POSTGRES_PASSWORD}@${POSTGRES_HOST}:5432/${POSTGRES_DB}
      - RAGFLOW_API_KEY=[REDACTED]
      - RAGFLOW_BASE_URL=${RAGFLOW_BASE_URL}
      - DASHSCOPE_API_KEY=[REDACTED]
      - APP_LOG_LEVEL=${APP_LOG_LEVEL}
      - LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=[REDACTED]
      - LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=[REDACTED]
      - LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=${LANGFUSE_BASE_URL}
      - PROMPT_LABEL=${PROMPT_LABEL}
      - DEFAULT_CASE_KB_IDS=${DEFAULT_CASE_KB_IDS}
      - DEFAULT_STAND_KB_IDS=${DEFAULT_STAND_KB_IDS}
      - MAX_TOOL_MESSAGES=${MAX_TOOL_MESSAGES}
      - MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS=${MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS}
      - MIN_CONTENT_THRESHOLD=${MIN_CONTENT_THRESHOLD}
      - MAX_RESEARCH_LOOPS=${MAX_RESEARCH_LOOPS}
      - MAX_AUDIT_LOOPS=${MAX_AUDIT_LOOPS}
      - JWT_SECRET=[REDACTED]${JWT_SECRET:-[REDACTED_DEFAULT]}
    logging:
      driver: "json-file"
      options:
        max-size: "50m"
        max-file: "3"
    networks:
      - archspec-net

  # 4. Database (PostgreSQL) || 本地调试使用独立环境
  postgres:
    image: pgvector/pgvector:pg15
    container_name: archspec-postgres
    environment:
      POSTGRES_DB: ${POSTGRES_DB}
      POSTGRES_USER: ${POSTGRES_USER}
      POSTGRES_PASSWORD: ${POSTGRES_PASSWORD}
      PGDATA: /var/lib/postgresql/data/pgdata
    ports:
      - "${POSTGRES_PORT}:5432"
    volumes:
      - postgres_data:/var/lib/postgresql/data
      # 自动初始化数据库脚本
      - ../../script/backend/initial_schema.sql:/docker-entrypoint-initdb.d/init.sql
    restart: always
    networks:
      - archspec-net
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "pg_isready -U ${POSTGRES_USER} -d ${POSTGRES_DB}"]
      interval: 10s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 5

networks:
  archspec-net:
    driver: bridge

volumes:
  postgres_data:

DOCKER_REGISTRY=registry.cn-guangzhou.aliyuncs.com/zzykj/
TAG=20260212

# --- PostgreSQL Database Configuration ---
POSTGRES_HOST=postgres
POSTGRES_PORT=5433
POSTGRES_DB=doc_generator
POSTGRES_USER=archspec
POSTGRES_PASSWORD=[REDACTED]

# --- Backend Service ---
SPRING_PROFILES_ACTIVE=prod
BACKEND_PORT=8080
# Backend DB Connection (Internal)
SPRING_DATASOURCE_URL=jdbc:postgresql://${POSTGRES_HOST}:5432/${POSTGRES_DB}?currentSchema=public
SPRING_DATASOURCE_USERNAME=${POSTGRES_USER}
SPRING_DATASOURCE_PASSWORD=[REDACTED]

# --- JWT 配置（Java 后端和 Python Agent 共享） ---
JWT_SECRET=[REDACTED]

# --- AI Agent Service ---
APP_LOG_LEVEL=DEBUG
AGENT_PORT=5000
# Agent PostgreSQL Connection
POSTGRES_URL=postgresql://${POSTGRES_USER}:${POSTGRES_PASSWORD}@${POSTGRES_HOST}:5432/${POSTGRES_DB}


# --- 阿里云 Dashscope API Key ---

DEFAULT_STAND_KB_IDS=["your_standard_kb_id_1", "your_standard_kb_id_2"]


# --- Langfuse 可观测性（可选） ---
# 注册地址: https://langfuse.com/ 或自部署
LANGFUSE_SECRET_KEY=[REDACTED]
LANGFUSE_PUBLIC_KEY=[REDACTED]
LANGFUSE_BASE_URL=http://your-langfuse-host:3000
PROMPT_LABEL=latest


# Workflow 配置 - Token 和长度限制
MAX_TOOL_MESSAGES=30           # 上下文中最多保留的 ToolMessage 数量
MAX_TOTAL_TOKENS=30000         # 最大 Token 数（预留 2k tokens 给响应）
MIN_CONTENT_THRESHOLD=1000     # 检索内容最小阈值（字符数）

# Workflow 配置 - 循环控制
MAX_RESEARCH_LOOPS=3           # Researcher 最大循环次数
MAX_AUDIT_LOOPS=2              # Auditor 最大循环次数

# --- Web Frontend ---
WEB_PORT=80

# --- Alibaba Cloud OSS Configuration (for review file upload) ---
OSS_ACCESS_KEY_ID=[REDACTED]
OSS_ACCESS_KEY_SECRET=[REDACTED]
OSS_STS_ROLE_ARN=acs:ram::1234567890123456:role/oss-upload-role
OSS_BUCKET=your-oss-bucket-name
OSS_REGION=cn-guangzhou
OSS_ENDPOINT=sts.cn-guangzhou.aliyuncs.com
OSS_HOST=https://your-oss-bucket.oss-cn-guangzhou.aliyuncs.com
OSS_UPLOAD_DIR=reviews/
OSS_EXPIRE_SECONDS=3600[END OF FILE]
